Rapid Knee OA Brace - Right | Single Upright Hinged | Lightweight & Breathable | Portable Support for Osteoarthritis and Rheumatoid Arthritis – universal size

PORTABLE COMFORT:  
Rapid Knee OA Brace - Right is designed with portability in mind, so you can take it with you wherever you go. It provides comfort and convenience throughout your day, whether at home or on the move.

BREATHABLE AND LIGHTWEIGHT:  
Crafted from breathable, non-allergenic materials, this knee brace offers superior comfort without compromising on breathability. Its lightweight design helps you stay cool and comfortable, even during extended wear.

ADJUSTABLE CONTROL AND SUPPORT:  
The Upright Hinged feature allows customized support tailored to your specific needs, helping you optimize your treatment regimen for maximum effectiveness.

USER-FRIENDLY DESIGN:  
The Rapid OA Knee - Right is engineered for ease of use, featuring an indexed hinge that allows for easy monitoring of range-of-motion (ROM) improvement. It offers dynamic or static setting options to provide versatility in usage as your rehabilitation needs change.

POLYCENTRIC ADJUSTABLE DESIGN:  
With its polycentric adjustable design, this knee brace offers flexibility in controlled motion and support, helping you manage osteoarthritis or rheumatoid arthritis-related knee issues with targeted bracing.

Actual Answer

Ultima Neo (TENS, EMS, IFC, Micro) w/ Li‑ion rechargeable battery – 28v strength

The Ultima Neo is an advanced multi‑mode electrotherapy stimulator. It combines four types of stimulation in one device:

- TENS (Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation)  
- EMS (Electrical Muscle Stimulation)  
- IFC (Interferential current)  
- Microcurrent  

Key points from PMT’s information:

- Device type: Dual‑channel electrotherapy stimulator  
- Modes included:  
  - Enhanced TENS modes  
  - Enhanced EMS modes  
  - Advanced Interferential (IFC) with sine wave technology  
  - State‑of‑the‑art Microcurrent mode  
- Interface: Includes body part diagrams to help select appropriate programs/modes  
- Power:  
  - Operates on a high‑capacity lithium‑ion rechargeable battery  
  - 28v strength  
  - The Li‑ion battery allows:  
    - Use of Interferential mode without being plugged into a wall  
    - Long battery run time on TENS, EMS, and Microcurrent modes  
- Channels: Dual channel (you can run two lead wires/sets of electrodes at once)  
- Package includes:  
  - Ultima Neo device  
  - Batteries  
  - Lead wires  
  - 1 pack of 4 electrodes  
  - Carrying case  
  - User’s manual  

If you tell me what you’d like to focus on (TENS vs EMS vs IFC vs Micro, or battery/strength), I can break that part down in more detail.

Actual Answer

NerveSpa Pro – 90 Day Supply (Product Code: NSFBPRO90)

PMT describes this as a clinical, advanced hand-and-foot neuropathy system designed for provider‑guided aquatic nerve stimulation over a 90‑day care window.

Key points:

- Purpose:
  - Helps relieve nerve discomfort, tingling, sensitivity, and pain in the hands and feet through repeated-use care plans.
  - Designed to support ongoing neuropathy care and long‑term nerve health.

- How it works:
  - Uses warm‑water immersion plus proprietary electrotherapy programming.
  - Combines biphasic and monophasic waveforms at 7.83 Hz (hybrid electrotherapy concept).
  - Aims to engage nerve-related tissue more broadly and circumferentially than surface pad-only systems.

- System design:
  - LCD touch screen device with built‑in microprocessor and programmed sequences.
  - Intended for clinic‑to‑home continuity, so providers can set up structured, repeated-use plans.

- Main benefits PMT highlights:
  - Relieves nerve discomfort and extremity pain with repeated use.
  - Supports broader nerve engagement in hands and feet than limited pad systems.
  - Warm water plus stimulation support circulation‑oriented care in the extremities.
  - Built for longer-term, structured use to support nerve function and circulation over time.
